  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Pacal, leader of The Maya (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Maya
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Atlatlist, Replacing Composite Bowman (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Unique Unit: Holkan, Replacing Pathfinder (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Pitz Court, Replacing Arena (Requires technology: Masonry)
        - Unique Improvement: Kuna (Unique Mayan Improvement.
          Can only be constructed in Forests or Jungles, and cannot be adjacent to another Kuna.); Requires technology: Construction
        - Civilization Ability: After researching Mathematics, receive a bonus Great Person at the end of every Maya Long Count cycle (every 394 years). Each bonus Great Person can only be chosen once.
      - Leader: Pacal
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the game.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - New Deal (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): New Deal: Landmarks and Great Person improvements produce +6 of their base yield types ( Manufactory/Citadel, Town, Academy, Embassy, Holy Site, Landmark), and +2 Tourism
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Arsenal of Democracy (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Arsenal of Democracy: +15% Production towards Military Units. +10 Influence with all known City-States when you expend Great People, and +40 Influence when you gift Units to City-States.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Economic Union (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Economic Union: Receive two additional Trade Routes. +6 Gold from trade routes with civilizations following Freedom.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 7
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 3
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 5
        - Boldness: 1
        - WarBias: 1
        - HostileBias: 2
        - WarmongerHate: 7

        - FriendlyBias: 7
        - GuardedBias: 9
        - AfraidBias: 8
        - DiplomaticBalance: 7
        - Friendliness: 6
        - WorkWithWillingness: 7
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 5
        - Loyalty: 9
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 5
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 5
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 3
        - MinorCivWarBias: 2
        - DenounceWillingness: 4
        - Forgiveness: 7
        - Meanness: 2
        - Neediness: 6
        - Chattiness: 5
        - DeceptiveBias: 4
        - Rationale: Tweaked by In-Game AI (DE-ESCALATION after nuclear crisis: Shoshone nuked Palenque (Event 44), Brazil has 3 Atomic Bombs + Jet Fighter + XCOM Squads at Antioch on our border (Events 30-37). Reducing VictoryCompetitiveness (9→7), Boldness (2→1), WarBias (2→1), HostileBias (3→2) - we cannot afford further provocation with two nuclear-armed superpowers. Increasing GuardedBias (8→9) and AfraidBias (7→8) - we must appear submissive to avoid being targeted again. FriendlyBias (6→7) and Friendliness (5→6) increase openness to diplomatic solutions. DenounceWillingness reduced (6→4) - avoiding verbal escalation. DeceptiveBias (5→4) - less manipulative, more honest about our weakness. Meanness reduced (3→2) - maximum diplomatic flexibility. This is survival mode: we need breathing room to recover from Palenque nuclear strike and Brazil's 82:1 military advantage.)
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Computers
        - Rationale: Continuing to Computers for trade route expansion (+1 route) and unlocking Telecommunications/Satellites. After Uxmal's nuclear devastation, our economy is crippled (-50 GPT deficit). The extra trade route is desperately needed for economic recovery. Computers also supports air infrastructure and reconnaissance capabilities critical against Brazil's nuclear-armed forces. This research path is essential for both Spaceship victory pursuit and survival against superior enemy air and nuclear capabilities. Mobile Tactics will follow for defensive unit upgrades (Mobile SAMs).
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: None (Policy)
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Normalizing Celt relationship to 0/0. Brazil's 5:1 military advantage (14,966 vs 2,804 for Celts) makes their membership in anti-Brazil coalition meaningless - they'll be eliminated in 7 turns regardless. Maintaining hostile stance (Public -5/Private -5) serves no strategic purpose. Formal normalization removes diplomatic friction with other coalition partners while we maintain focus on Shoshone cultural threat and Brazil's emerging nuclear danger. Not increasing to positive given their weakness.
        - UpdatedTurn: 397
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Normalizing Ottoman relationship to 0/0. Ottomans are Brazil's vassal (Order ideology capitulated) but hostility serves no purpose given the power dynamics (Brazil 14,966 vs Ottomans 3,578). We cannot meaningfully harm them through Brazil, and maintaining negative modifiers creates unnecessary diplomatic complications. Full normalization to 0/0 removes strategic distraction from real threats: Brazil's nuclear capability and Shoshone's cultural victory. Keeping neutral stance, not positive given their Brazil alignment.
        - UpdatedTurn: 397
        
        ### Byzantium
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Maintaining maximum alliance with Byzantium (30 turns elapsed since last update but relationship remains strategic priority). Byzantium is CRITICAL for World Congress: (1) 6 delegates needed next turn to vote against Brazil's 'World Ideology: Order' proposal; (2) Shares Freedom ideology with us, vulnerable to Brazil's Order pressure; (3) Defensive Pact honors if Brazil or Shoshone attack; (4) Fighting Brazil (War Score -60%) and Shoshone, shared strategic interests. Byzantium is our most important ally - without their votes, Brazil's Order ideology will pass, undermining our diplomatic position and potentially flipping Byzantium. Maximum relationship ensures Byzantium honors our alliance and votes with us against Brazil's growing hegemony.
        - UpdatedTurn: 415
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: 15
        - Private: -10
        - Rationale: Slightly increasing public stance toward Brazil (25→15) while maintaining limited private hostility (-10). Brazil's military advantage is now 82:1 (112K vs 1.4K) and they have 3 Atomic Bombs positioned at Antioch - a few tiles from Chichen Itza, Palenque, and Uxmal. Further public antagonism risks immediate nuclear retaliation after Shoshone already nuked Palenque. Public de-escalation to 15 signals submission without capitulation. Private hostility at -10 acknowledges they just killed 5 Anti-Aircraft Guns this turn (Events 12-16) and maintain nuclear threats on our border. This asymmetric signaling: public appeasement to avoid being a nuclear target, private guardedness reflecting the reality they hold existential leverage over us. We cannot fight back - must buy time.
        - UpdatedTurn: 427
        
        ### The Shoshone
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -10
        - Rationale: Further reducing hostility toward Shoshone (-15→-10) while maintaining guarded stance. Shoshone just NUKED PALenque (Event 44) - direct nuclear attack on our capital. However, with Brazil's 3 Atomic Bombs on our northern border and Shoshone's nuclear capability established, we cannot afford to be completely isolated between two nuclear powers. Reducing public hostility may prevent further nuclear strikes - Shoshone showed willingness to use nukes, and public de-escalation might signal we're not worth further nuclear expenditure. Private hostility at -10 acknowledges the nuclear attack on Palenque but reduced from -15 reflects survival priority. We must appear weak and non-threatening to both superpowers.
        - UpdatedTurn: 427
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: MAXIMIZING Assyria relationship to 85/90 (up from 85/85). CRITICAL: After Uxmal nuclear strike, we need Aluminum NOW for Mobile SAMs and air defense. Assyria has 17 Aluminum - our ONLY viable source. Brazil's 89:1 military advantage means we cannot expand territory. Higher relationship signals maximum priority and improves trade terms. Assyria is at war with Shoshone (shared enemy) and neutral with Brazil - critical buffer. With our civilization under nuclear attack and economy collapsing, this relationship is existential. Maximum reasonable relationship to ensure successful Aluminum trade for rebuilding anti-air capabilities against Brazil's nuclear threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 430
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: CRITICAL: Brazil just nuked Uxmal, reducing population from 29 to 14 - devastating loss. Maintaining survival strategy with adjustments: Gold raised to 85 (economy in deficit -50 GPT, need for purchases), Defense/CityDefense/Antiair at MAX (Brazil nuclear threat persists), Production at 100 (rebuilding Uxmal and defenses), Diplomacy at 85 (urgent need Aluminum from Assyria, World Congress in 8 turns), Air raised to 45 (build bombers for retaliation capability), Increased UseNuke to 75 (deterrence priority). Mobile SAM at TikAL completes in 2 turns - critical for air defense. Fallout cleanup needs TileImprovement 25. Mobile Tactics in 18 turns - defensive tech. Spaceship priority remains despite devastation (only viable win path now with military inferiority). Uxmal's Heavy Bomber may not complete before another attack - accepting risk. Science 95 maintains tech race. Happiness 100 critical - 42% Unhappy causes combat penalties.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        
        ### Flavors

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 45
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 7 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: 360
          - Shoshone Sphere of Influence: Sofia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: The Celts:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: 382
          - Open Door: Copenhagen:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Ottoman Sphere of Influence: Colombo:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Segou:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Ottoman Sphere of Influence: Ouagadougou:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: 426
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 415
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 371
          - Ottoman Sphere of Influence: Melbourne:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Shoshone Sphere of Influence: Onondaga:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 393
          - Open Door: Sokoto:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 360

            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Shoshone Sphere of Influence: Ormus:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 349
          - World Religion: Buddhism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: International Space Station:
            - Description: Begins the International Space Station project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : International Space Station wonder appears in Capital. (+1 Production from Scientists, and +1 Science from Engineers. Great Scientists provide 33% more Science when used to discover new Technology. +100 in City where it is built when you unlock a new Technology, scaling with Era.)
              : Free Great Scientist.
              : One time Research boost.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Rocketry.
            - Proposer: Brazil

        ### 107

        You, Pacal (leader of The Maya, Player 4), are making strategic decisions after turn 430.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Pacal, leader of The Maya (Opponent 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Maya
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Atlatlist, Replacing Composite Bowman (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Unique Unit: Holkan, Replacing Pathfinder (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Pitz Court, Replacing Arena (Requires technology: Masonry)
        - Unique Improvement: Kuna (Unique Mayan Improvement.
          Can only be constructed in Forests or Jungles, and cannot be adjacent to another Kuna.); Requires technology: Construction
        - Civilization Ability: After researching Mathematics, receive a bonus Great Person at the end of every Maya Long Count cycle (every 394 years). Each bonus Great Person can only be chosen once.
      - Leader: Pacal
      - OpponentID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the world.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - Universal Healthcare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Universal Healthcare: Receive a free Hospital in every City. +50 when a Citizen is born in any City, scaling with Era.
      - New Deal (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): New Deal: Landmarks and Great Person improvements produce +6 of their base yield types ( Manufactory/Citadel, Town, Academy, Embassy, Holy Site, Landmark), and +2 Tourism
      - Transnationalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Transnationalism: Each turn, increases the chance that a Corporate Franchise will appear in a foreign City to 15% (normally 5%). Global Franchise maximum increased by 25%, and every owned Town you have built counts as a Franchise. +2 Culture from all Corporation Offices.
      - Their Finest Hour (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Their Finest Hour: All cities get +2 Air Unit Slots. Each Air Unit stationed in a city increases the City's Defense by 3. Can build B17 Bombers.
      - Arsenal of Democracy (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Arsenal of Democracy: +15% Production towards Military Units. +10 Influence with all known City-States when you expend Great People, and +40 Influence when you gift Units to City-States.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Self Determination (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Self Determination: Liberating a city (including removing another civ's Sphere of Influence on a City-State through conquest) gives 15 XP to all units, 50 Influence with all City-States, and 40 Science, scaling with Era and City Population. The liberated city gains an Arsenal and 6 units.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Opponents lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Economic Union (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Economic Union: Receive two additional Trade Routes. +6 Gold from trade routes with civilizations following Freedom.
      - Shadow Networks (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 25% of Science generated by Allied City-States.
        - * +3% Culture in Capital for every 100 Spy Points ever accumulated (up to 30%).
        - * +3 Science from Constabularies and Police Stations.
        - * +1 Science from Specialists.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - TargetID: 0
        - Rationale: We are facing a catastrophic military deficit against Brazil (108k vs 927) and are at extreme risk of annihilation. We need to force our diplomatic stance to be as friendly and open as possible to encourage a peace treaty immediately and save our empire.
        - Private: 100
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: The Shoshone have besieged Palenque and are destroying our forces. We must set our relationship to friendly to encourage our staff to make peace immediately, stop the war weariness, and save our capital.
        - TargetID: 2
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: The Ottomans are at war with us as a vassal of Brazil. We seek to normalize relations and achieve peace to reduce overall war weariness and secure our borders.
        - TargetID: 3
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: The Celts are at war with us as a vassal of Brazil. We seek peace to end the multi-front war and stabilize our empire.
        - TargetID: 6
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-persona
      - args:
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 5
        - Boldness: 1
        - AfraidBias: 10
        - WarBias: 1
        - Forgiveness: 10
        - FriendlyBias: 10
        - GuardedBias: 3
        - DeceptiveBias: 1
        - Meanness: 1
        - Friendliness: 10
        - Loyalty: 10
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Rationale: We are adjusting our persona to be extremely submissive, peace-seeking, friendly, and communicative. Facing a 100x stronger coalition of enemies, we must prioritize peaceful war resolution (DiplomaticBalance), maximize forgiveness, and lower meanness to accept any peace offer, while using higher chattiness to initiate peace negotiations.
        - HostileBias: 1
        - Chattiness: 8
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Economic Union
        - Rationale: We have a severe gold deficit of -95 GPT and 6 unused trade routes due to the Cold War resolution. Economic Union will grant us 2 additional trade routes and +6 Gold per trade route with Freedom civilizations (Byzantium), which is our only viable trade partner, saving our economy.
    - #6:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: Computers will grant us an additional Trade Route, which is crucial for our survival and economic recovery under the Cold War resolution. It also leads towards advanced tech.
        - Technology: Computers
    - #7:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - CityDefense: 100
          - Production: 100
          - Antiair: 100
          - Gold: 100
          - Espionage: 95
          - Happiness: 100
          - Spaceship: 90
          - Defense: 100
          - Offense: 0
          - Air: 60
          - Science: 100
          - Mobilization: 50
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
      - rationale: We pivot our civilization to a survival and recovery state. Gold and Happiness are prioritized at 100 to combat our -95 GPT deficit and combat unhappiness. Defense and Antiair are maximized to protect Palenque and Tikal from the massive Shoshone and Brazilian forces, while continuing our long-term science focus (Spaceship, Science, Production) to achieve victory once peace is secured.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Game Status**
            
            I'm currently evaluating our position in this late-game scenario. With 430 turns elapsed and civilizations experiencing varied technological eras, I'm analyzing our overall global and local standing as the Maya.
            
            **Addressing Financial Crisis**
            
            I am prioritizing immediate solutions to our severe gold deficit, which is impacting unit and science generation. Concurrently, I'm assessing our precarious diplomatic standing, particularly the imminent cultural victory threat from Brazil.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Landscape**
            
            My focus has shifted to the alarming diplomatic situation. Brazil is on the cusp of a Diplomatic victory with 29 delegates, and has allied with the Celts and Ottomans, who are also at war with us. My immediate priority is counteracting their influence and managing our numerous ongoing conflicts.
            
            **Revising Science Path**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our science trajectory. While our grand strategy remains "Spaceship," the current research queue is suboptimal. I'm clarifying how the "set-research" tool functions and assessing the immediate next steps to ensure we're progressing efficiently towards our victory condition, prioritizing future technologies.
            
            **Adjusting Research Strategy**
            
            I am re-evaluating our science path, specifically considering the tactical advantage of the Stealth Bomber unlocked by Stealth. I'm also examining available policy options, noting our next policy is due in 29 turns, and considering immediate acquisitions like "New Deal" or "Civil Society" to bolster our position.
            
            **Investigating Gold Shortage**
            
            I'm analyzing our severe gold deficit. It appears our lack of external trade routes, due to the active Cold War and ideological restrictions, is the primary cause, with only internal routes currently active.
            
            **Exploring Trade Solutions**
            
            I'm focusing on resolving our gold shortage by assessing trade options. It appears Byzantium is our only viable external trade partner due to ideological alignment, and I'm evaluating the "Economic Union" policy to maximize trade route benefits.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Posture**
            
            I'm exploring ways to leverage the `set-relationship` tool to improve peace acceptance by increasing positive diplomatic modifiers towards potential enemies like Brazil and the Shoshone. This is critical given our overwhelming military inferiority, which I'm also investigating, as our current unit count of 14 is alarmingly low.
            
            **Securing Peace Agreements**
            
            I'm prioritizing immediate peace negotiations with Brazil and the Shoshone, given our dire military situation and their overwhelming forces. I need to confirm how the `set-relationship` tool influences peace acceptance for both parties before implementing it.
            
            **Negotiating Peace Treaties**
            
            I'm actively pursuing peace with Brazil, the Shoshone, Celts, and Ottomans. My current focus is leveraging the `set-relationship` tool to achieve this, prioritizing immediate de-escalation given our critical military disadvantage and the pressing need to lift sieges and reduce war weariness across the empire.
            
            **Adjusting AI Personalities**
            
            I'm considering adjusting our AI's persona to be more peace-seeking. By lowering "meanness" and increasing "friendliness" and "loyalty," I believe we can foster better diplomatic outcomes. This aligns with our immediate need to de-escalate conflicts.
            
            **Revising Grand Strategy**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our Grand Strategy. While "Spaceship" remains our long-term goal, the current war necessitates a short-term focus on survival. I'm weighing how to best balance our flavors, prioritizing defense, production, and science amidst ongoing conflicts.
            
            **Adjusting Unit Focus**
            
            I'm reconsidering our unit priorities given our defensive AI settings. With high scores in MilitaryTraining, Antiair, and Production, I'm now focused on enhancing our Gold and Science generation, and evaluating policies like "Economic Union" and "Their Finest Hour" to further optimize our strategy.
            
            **Implementing Economic Union**
            
            I am prioritizing the "Economic Union" policy to address our severe gold deficit. With Byzantium closely situated and following Freedom, this policy's boost to trade routes will be critical for economic recovery. I'm also reviewing our research path, considering an immediate shift to "Stealth" for its defensive advantages.
            
            **Prioritizing Trade Route**
            
            I've identified "Computers" as a critical research target. The immediate benefit of an additional trade route is precisely what we need to alleviate our gold shortage. My spies are also positioned effectively across various cities and for counter-intelligence missions.
            
            **Leveraging Diplomatic Assets**
            
            I've just acquired a Great Diplomat and am strategizing its deployment for maximum impact. Concurrently, I'm preparing to utilize the `set-relationship` tool to foster peace with our current adversaries, Brazil and the Shoshone, initiating our peace negotiation efforts.
            
            **Confirming AI Parameters**
            
            I am verifying the exact available policies and confirming that "Economic Union" is indeed an option I can implement. My focus is also on solidifying our research into "Computers" to secure that vital trade route, ensuring our overall strategy remains on track.
            
            **Setting AI Flavors**
            
            I am configuring the AI's flavors to prioritize gold, science, production, happiness, and defense, while minimizing offensive capabilities. This strategic adjustment aims to address immediate economic needs and bolster our resilience against ongoing conflicts.
            
            **Adjusting AI Behaviors**
            
            I'm fine-tuning the AI's persona by reducing aggression and prioritizing friendly biases. My goal is to maximize our willingness to yield for peace, especially given the vast military disparity, while still focusing on defense and recovery.
            
            **Configuring AI Persona**
            
            I am configuring the AI's persona to be significantly more peace-oriented. By adjusting traits like "Meanness," "Friendliness," and "Forgiveness" to high values, and reducing "DeceptiveBias," I am aiming to facilitate easier peace negotiations with all current adversaries.
            
            **Adjusting Ideological Beliefs**
            
            I'm clarifying our ideology; we are indeed Freedom, despite a tooltip suggesting otherwise. My focus is now on confirming our relationship modifiers with all current adversaries like Brazil, Shoshone, Ottomans, and Celts, before employing the `set-relationship` tool to facilitate peace negotiations.
